?generate-app-report.js13.3 KB
#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

/*
 * ChitraGupt � generate-app-report.js
 * Builds a single self-contained HTML report of the whole project.
 */

const argv       = process.argv.slice(2);
const positional = argv.filter(a => !a.startsWith('--'));
const ROOT       = path.resolve(positional[0] || process.cwd());
const OUT        = path.resolve(positional[1] || path.join(ROOT, 'app-report.html'));
const INCLUDE_ENV = argv.includes('--include-env');

if (!fs.existsSync(ROOT)) {
  console.error('Directory not found: ' + ROOT);
  process.exit(1);
}

const SKIP_DIRS = new Set([
  'node_modules', '.git', '.expo', 'dist', 'web-build', '.next', 'build',
  '__pycache__', '.venv', 'venv', '.pnpm-store', '.turbo', '.cache',
  'coverage', 'example', '.pytest_cache', '.mypy_cache', '.ruff_cache',
  '.parcel-cache', '.idea', '.vscode', '.DS_Store', 'out', 'tmp', 'logs',
  'vendor', 'storage'
]);

const SKIP_EXTS = new Set([
  '.png','.jpg','.jpeg','.gif','.webp','.bmp','.tiff','.ico','.icns',
  '.pdf','.zip','.tar','.gz','.tgz','.7z','.rar','.bz2',
  '.mp3','.mp4','.mov','.avi','.mkv','.wav','.flac','.ogg',
  '.woff','.woff2','.ttf','.otf','.eot',
  '.db','.sqlite','.sqlite3','.mdb',
  '.pyc','.pyo','.class','.o','.a','.so','.dll','.dylib','.exe','.bin','.node',
  '.lock','.pdb','.map'
]);

const MAX_FILE_BYTES = 300 * 1024;

const escapeHtml = (s) => String(s)
  .replace(/&/g,'&amp;')
  .replace(/</g,'&lt;')
  .replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
  .replace(/"/g,'&quot;')
  .replace(/'/g,'&#39;');

const fmtSize = (b) =>
  b < 1024      ? b + ' B'
: b < 1048576   ? (b/1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB'
                : (b/1048576).toFixed(2) + ' MB';

const getExt = (n) => {
  const l = n.toLowerCase();
  if (l.startsWith('.') && l.indexOf('.', 1) === -1) return l;
  const i = l.lastIndexOf('.');
  return i === -1 ? '(none)' : l.slice(i);
};

const allFiles = [];

function walk(dir, relBase) {
  relBase = relBase || '';
  let entries;
  try { entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }); }
  catch (e) { return; }

  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) continue;
    const name = entry.name;
    const full = path.join(dir, name);
    const rel  = relBase ? relBase + '/' + name : name;

    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      if (SKIP_DIRS.has(name)) continue;
      walk(full, rel);
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      if (path.resolve(full) === OUT) continue;

      const isEnv = name === '.env'
        || (name.startsWith('.env.') && name !== '.env.example');
      if (isEnv && !INCLUDE_ENV) continue;

      const ext = path.extname(name).toLowerCase();
      if (SKIP_EXTS.has(ext)) continue;

      let stat;
      try { stat = fs.statSync(full); } catch (e) { continue; }

      allFiles.push({ rel, full, name, size: stat.size, ext: getExt(name) });
    }
  }
}

console.error('Walking ' + ROOT + ' ...');
walk(ROOT);
console.error('Found ' + allFiles.length + ' files.');

const totalSize  = allFiles.reduce((s, f) => s + f.size, 0);
const topFolders = new Set(
  allFiles.map(f => f.rel.includes('/') ? f.rel.split('/')[0] : '(root)')
).size;

const extStats = new Map();
for (const f of allFiles) {
  if (!extStats.has(f.ext)) extStats.set(f.ext, { count: 0, size: 0 });
  const e = extStats.get(f.ext);
  e.count++;
  e.size += f.size;
}
const extSorted = [...extStats.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1].count - a[1].count);

function buildTree(files) {
  const root = { name: '', type: 'dir', children: new Map() };
  for (const f of files) {
    const parts = f.rel.split('/');
    let node = root;
    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
      const p = parts[i];
      if (!node.children.has(p))
        node.children.set(p, { name: p, type: 'dir', children: new Map() });
      node = node.children.get(p);
    }
    node.children.set(parts[parts.length - 1],
      { name: parts[parts.length - 1], type: 'file' });
  }
  return root;
}

function renderTreeLines(node, prefix) {
  prefix = prefix || '';
  const lines = [];
  const entries = [...node.children.values()].sort((a, b) =>
    a.type !== b.type ? (a.type === 'dir' ? -1 : 1)
                      : a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  entries.forEach((child, idx) => {
    const isLast    = idx === entries.length - 1;
    const connector = isLast ? '+-- ' : '+-- ';
    const next      = prefix + (isLast ? '    ' : '�   ');
    if (child.type === 'dir') {
      lines.push({ text: prefix + connector + child.name + '/', type: 'dir' });
      lines.push(...renderTreeLines(child, next));
    } else {
      lines.push({ text: prefix + connector + child.name, type: 'file' });
    }
  });
  return lines;
}

const treeRoot  = buildTree(allFiles);
const treeLines = renderTreeLines(treeRoot);

function groupByTop(files) {
  const groups = new Map();
  for (const f of files) {
    const top = f.rel.includes('/') ? f.rel.split('/')[0] : '(root)';
    if (!groups.has(top)) groups.set(top, []);
    groups.get(top).push(f);
  }
  return [...groups.entries()].sort((a, b) =>
    a[0] === '(root)' ? -1 :
    b[0] === '(root)' ?  1 :
    a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));
}
const groups = groupByTop(allFiles);

function buildFileCard(file) {
  let content = '';
  let note    = '';
  try {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(file.full);
    let isBinary = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(buf.length, 8192); i++) {
      if (buf[i] === 0) { isBinary = true; break; }
    }
    if (isBinary) {
      return '<details class="file-card"><summary>'
        + '<span class=\'chev\'>?</span>'
        + '<span class=\'file-path\'>' + escapeHtml(file.rel) + '</span>'
        + '<span class=\'file-size\'>' + fmtSize(file.size) + ' � binary</span>'
        + '</summary><pre class=\'code\'>[binary file � contents not shown]</pre></details>';
    }
    if (buf.length > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {
      content = buf.slice(0, MAX_FILE_BYTES).toString('utf8');
      note = '\n\n� [truncated � full file is ' + fmtSize(file.size)
           + ', showing first ' + fmtSize(MAX_FILE_BYTES) + ']';
    } else {
      content = buf.toString('utf8');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    content = '[error reading file: ' + err.message + ']';
  }
  return '<details class="file-card"><summary>'
    + '<span class=\'chev\'>?</span>'
    + '<span class=\'file-path\'>' + escapeHtml(file.rel) + '</span>'
    + '<span class=\'file-size\'>' + fmtSize(file.size) + '</span>'
    + '</summary><pre class=\'code\'>'
    + escapeHtml(content + note)
    + '</pre></details>';
}

const now      = new Date();
const dateStr  = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const timeStr  = now.toISOString().slice(11, 16);

const treeHtml = treeLines
  .map(l => '<span class=\'' + l.type + '\'>' + escapeHtml(l.text) + '</span>')
  .join('\n');

const extHtml = extSorted.slice(0, 20).map(([ext, s]) =>
  '<div class=\'ext\'>'
  + '<div class=\'code\'>'   + escapeHtml(ext)  + '</div>'
  + '<div class=\'count\'>'  + s.count          + '</div>'
  + '<div class=\'size\'>'   + fmtSize(s.size)  + '</div>'
  + '</div>'
).join('\n');

const sourceHtml = groups.map(([groupName, files]) => {
  const cards = files
    .sort((a, b) => a.rel.localeCompare(b.rel))
    .map(buildFileCard)
    .join('\n');
  return '<div class="group">'
    + '<div class=\'group-header\'>'
    + '<span class=\'group-name\'>'  + escapeHtml(groupName) + '</span>'
    + '<span class=\'group-count\'>' + files.length
      + ' file' + (files.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>'
    + '</div>'
    + cards
    + '</div>';
}).join('\n');

const html = '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head>'
+ '<meta charset="utf-8">'
+ '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
+ '<title>CampusPlace � Complete Source Code Report</title>'
+ '<style>'

## About Laravel

Laravel is a web application framework with expressive, elegant syntax. We believe development must be an enjoyable and creative experience to be truly fulfilling. Laravel takes the pain out of development by easing common tasks used in many web projects, such as:

- [Simple, fast routing engine](https://laravel.com/docs/routing).
- [Powerful dependency injection container](https://laravel.com/docs/container).
- Multiple back-ends for [session](https://laravel.com/docs/session) and [cache](https://laravel.com/docs/cache) storage.
- Expressive, intuitive [database ORM](https://laravel.com/docs/eloquent).
- Database agnostic [schema migrations](https://laravel.com/docs/migrations).
- [Robust background job processing](https://laravel.com/docs/queues).
- [Real-time event broadcasting](https://laravel.com/docs/broadcasting).

Laravel is accessible, powerful, and provides tools required for large, robust applications.

## Learning Laravel

Laravel has the most extensive and thorough [documentation](https://laravel.com/docs) and video tutorial library of all modern web application frameworks, making it a breeze to get started with the framework. You can also check out [Laravel Learn](https://laravel.com/learn), where you will be guided through building a modern Laravel application.

If you don't feel like reading, [Laracasts](https://laracasts.com) can help. Laracasts contains thousands of video tutorials on a range of topics including Laravel, modern PHP, unit testing, and JavaScript. Boost your skills by digging into our comprehensive video library.

?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/AuthenticatedSessionController.php1.0 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the login view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.login');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
     */
    public function store(LoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->authenticate();

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    /**
     * Destroy an authenticated session.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();

        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/ConfirmablePasswordController.php1.0 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the confirm password view.
     */
    public function show(): View
    {
        return view('auth.confirm-password');
    }

    /**
     * Confirm the user's password.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
            'email' => $request->user()->email,
            'password' => $request->password,
        ])) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'password' => __('auth.password'),
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/CustomRegistrationController.php6.7 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\Student;
use App\Models\Company;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\AicteVerification;
use App\Models\DpdpConsent;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class CustomRegistrationController extends Controller
{
    // ==========================================
    // STUDENT REGISTRATION
    // ==========================================
    public function showStudentForm()
    {
        $colleges = College::where('status', 'verified')->get();
        return view('auth.register-student', compact('colleges'));
    }

    public function registerStudent(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|email|unique:users,email',
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
            'dpdp_consent' => 'required|accepted',
            'college_id' => 'required|exists:colleges,id',
            'enrollment_no' => 'required|string|unique:students,enrollment_no',
            'department_id' => 'required|exists:departments,id',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request) {
            $user = User::create([
                'name' => $request->name,
                'email' => $request->email,
                'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                'role' => 'student',
                'status' => 'active',
            ]);

            DpdpConsent::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'consent_type' => 'data_collection',
                'consented' => true,
                'ip_address' => $request->ip(),
                'consented_at' => now(),
            ]);

            Student::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'college_id' => $request->college_id,
                'department_id' => $request->department_id,
                'enrollment_no' => $request->enrollment_no,
                'status' => 'active',
            ]);

            event(new Registered($user));
            Auth::login($user);
        });

        return redirect()->route('student.dashboard')->with('success', 'Registration successful. Welcome!');
    }

    // ==========================================
    // COMPANY REGISTRATION
    // ==========================================
    public function showCompanyForm()
    {
        return view('auth.register-company');
    }

    public function registerCompany(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|email|unique:users,email',
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
            'dpdp_consent' => 'required|accepted',
            'company_name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'industry' => 'required|string',
            'hr_name' => 'required|string',
            'hr_phone' => 'required|string',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request) {
            $user = User::create([
                'name' => $request->name,
                'email' => $request->email,
                'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                'role' => 'company',
                'status' => 'active',
            ]);

            DpdpConsent::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'consent_type' => 'data_collection',
                'consented' => true,
                'ip_address' => $request->ip(),
                'consented_at' => now(),
            ]);

            Company::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'name' => $request->company_name,
                'industry' => $request->industry,
                'hr_name' => $request->hr_name,
                'hr_email' => $request->email,
                'hr_phone' => $request->hr_phone,
                'status' => 'pending', // Requires Super Admin approval
            ]);

            event(new Registered($user));
            Auth::login($user);
        });

        return redirect()->route('company.dashboard')->with('success', 'Registration successful. Please wait for Super Admin approval.');
    }

    // ==========================================
    // COLLEGE REGISTRATION (Apply)
    // ==========================================
    public function showCollegeForm()
    {
        $states = \App\Models\State::all();
        return view('auth.register-college', compact('states'));
    }

    public function registerCollege(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|email|unique:users,email|unique:colleges,email',
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
            'college_name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'college_code' => 'required|string|unique:colleges,code',
            'state_id' => 'required|exists:states,id',
            'city' => 'required|string',
            'tpo_name' => 'required|string',
            'aicte_certificate' => 'required|mimes:pdf|max:5120',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request) {
            $user = User::create([
                'name' => $request->name, // TPO Name
                'email' => $request->email,
                'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                'role' => 'college_admin',
                'status' => 'active',
            ]);

            // Handle Document Upload
            $path = $request->file('aicte_certificate')->store('verification-docs', 'public');

            $college = College::create([
                'state_id' => $request->state_id,
                'name' => $request->college_name,
                'code' => $request->college_code,
                'email' => $request->email,
                'city' => $request->city,
                'tpo_name' => $request->tpo_name,
                'status' => 'pending', // Requires Super Admin approval
            ]);

            AicteVerification::create([
                'college_id' => $college->id,
                'certificate_url' => $path,
                'registration_number' => $request->college_code,
                'status' => 'pending'
            ]);

            // DO NOT LOGIN. Send them to login page to wait for approval.
        });

        return redirect()->route('login')->with('status', 'College application submitted. Please wait for Super Admin verification.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class EmailVerificationNotificationController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Send a new email verification notification.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
        }

        $request->user()->sendEmailVerificationNotification();

        return back()->with('status', 'verification-link-sent');
    }
}

?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php2.2 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class NewPasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset view.
     */
    public function create(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('auth.reset-password', ['request' => $request]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming new password request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => ['required'],
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function (User $user) use ($request) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
                ])->save();

                event(new PasswordReset($user));
            }
        );

        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
        return $status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET
                    ? redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordController.php795 B
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class PasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Update the user's password.
     */
    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validateWithBag('updatePassword', [
            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update([
            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        ]);

        return back()->with('status', 'password-updated');
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordResetLinkController.php1.3 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset link request view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.forgot-password');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
        ]);

        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
            $request->only('email')
        );

        return $status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT
                    ? back()->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/RegisteredUserController.php1.6 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\DpdpConsent;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
{
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.register');
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'lowercase', 'email', 'max:255', 'unique:'.User::class],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
            'dpdp_consent' => ['required', 'accepted'], // Mandatory consent
        ]);

        $user = User::create([
            'name' => $request->name,
            'email' => $request->email,
            'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
            'role' => 'student', // Default role for public registration
        ]);

        // Save DPDP Consent
        DpdpConsent::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'consent_type' => 'data_collection',
            'consented' => true,
            'ip_address' => $request->ip(),
            'consented_at' => now(),
        ]);

        event(new Registered($user));

        Auth::login($user);

        return redirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Auth/VerifyEmailController.php800 B
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;

class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
     */
    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
        }

        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
            event(new Verified($request->user()));
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\CollegeAdmin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Company;
use App\Models\CompanyCollegeBlacklist;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class BlacklistController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $collegeId = auth()->user()->collegeAdminProfile->college_id;
        
        // Get all companies that are globally approved
        $companies = Company::where('status', 'approved')->get();
        
        // Get companies blacklisted by this college
        $blacklisted = CompanyCollegeBlacklist::where('college_id', $collegeId)->with('company')->latest()->get();
        
        return view('college-admin.blacklist.index', compact('companies', 'blacklisted'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'company_id' => 'required|exists:companies,id',
            'reason' => 'required|string|min:10'
        ]);

        $collegeId = auth()->user()->collegeAdminProfile->college_id;

        // Check if already blacklisted by this college
        $exists = CompanyCollegeBlacklist::where('company_id', $request->company_id)->where('college_id', $collegeId)->exists();
        if ($exists) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Company is already blacklisted for your college.');
        }

        CompanyCollegeBlacklist::create([
            'company_id' => $request->company_id,
            'college_id' => $collegeId,
            'reason' => $request->reason,
            'blacklisted_by' => auth()->id()
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Company blacklisted for your college successfully.');
    }

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Company;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Application;
use App\Models\Interview;
use App\Models\Offer;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class InterviewController extends Controller
{
    public function create(Application $application)
    {
        // Security check
        if ($application->job->company_id !== auth()->user()->companyProfile->id) { abort(403); }
        return view('company.interviews.create', compact('application'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request, Application $application)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'scheduled_at' => 'required|date|after:now',
            'mode' => 'required|in:online,offline',
            'meeting_link' => 'nullable|url|required_if:mode,online',
            'venue' => 'nullable|string|required_if:mode,offline',
        ]);

        Interview::create([
            'application_id' => $application->id,
            'round_number' => 1,
            'round_name' => 'Technical Round',
            'mode' => $request->mode,
            'scheduled_at' => $request->scheduled_at,
            'duration_minutes' => 60,
            'meeting_link' => $request->meeting_link,
            'venue' => $request->venue,
            'result' => 'pending',
        ]);

        // Update application status
        $application->update(['status' => 'interview']);

        return redirect()->route('company.jobs.applicants', $application->job_id)
                         ->with('success', 'Interview scheduled successfully.');
    }

    public function releaseOffer(Application $application)
    {
        // Security check
        if ($application->job->company_id !== auth()->user()->companyProfile->id) { abort(403); }

        // Create Offer
        Offer::create([
            'student_id' => $application->student_id,
            'company_id' => $application->job->company_id,
            'job_id' => $application->job_id,
            'college_id' => $application->college_id,
            'package' => $application->job->package_max,
            'status' => 'released',
            'acceptance_deadline' => now()->addDays(7),
        ]);

        // Update application status
        $application->update(['status' => 'selected']);
        
        // Update student status
        $application->student->update(['status' => 'placed']);

        return back()->with('success', 'Offer released successfully!');
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/Company/JobController.php3.5 KB
<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Company;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Job;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\Application;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class JobController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $company = auth()->user()->companyProfile;
        $jobs = $company->jobs()->latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('company.jobs.index', compact('jobs'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $colleges = College::where('status', 'verified')->get();
        return view('company.jobs.create', compact('colleges'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'title' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'required|string',
            'job_type' => 'required|in:full_time,internship',
            'package_min' => 'required|numeric',
            'package_max' => 'required|numeric',
            'deadline' => 'required|date|after:today',
            'colleges' => 'required|array|min:1|max:1000',
            'colleges.*' => 'exists:colleges,id',
        ]);

        $company = auth()->user()->companyProfile;

        // Handle Skills Array
        $requiredSkills = [];
        if ($request->required_skills) {
            $requiredSkills = array_map('trim', explode(',', $request->required_skills));
        }

        $job = Job::create([
            'company_id' => $company->id,
            'title' => $request->title,
            'description' => $request->description,
            'job_type' => $request->job_type,
            'package_min' => $request->package_min,
            'package_max' => $request->package_max,
            'location' => $request->location,
            'eligibility' => [
                'cgpa' => $request->cgpa ?? 0,
                'backlogs' => $request->backlogs ?? 99,
                'passing_year' => $request->passing_year,
                'departments' => $request->departments ?? [],
                'skills' => $requiredSkills,
            ],
            'deadline' => $request->deadline,
            'status' => 'active',
        ]);

        // Map colleges
        $job->colleges()->attach($request->colleges, ['approval_status' => 'approved', 'approved_at' => now()]); 

        return redirect()->route('company.jobs.index')->with('success', 'Job posted successfully.');
    }

    public function applicants(Job $job)
    {
        if ($job->company_id !== auth()->user()->companyProfile->id) { abort(403); }
        $applications = $job->applications()->with(['student.user', 'student.college', 'student.department'])->latest()->paginate(15);
        return view('company.jobs.applicants', compact('job', 'applications'));
    }

    public function updateApplicationStatus(Request $request, Application $application)
    {
        $request->validate(['status' => 'required|in:shortlisted,rejected']);
        $application->update(['status' => $request->status]);
        
        // Notify Student
        \App\Models\Notification::create([
            'user_id' => $application->student->user_id,
            'title' => 'Application Update: ' . ucfirst($request->status),
            'message' => 'Your application for ' . $application->job->title . ' at ' . $application->job->company->name . ' has been marked as ' . $request->status . '.',
            'type' => 'shortlist',
            'is_critical' => true,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', "Application marked as {$request->status}.");
    }
}

?app/Http/Controllers/ProfileController.php1.4 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Requests\ProfileUpdateRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Redirect;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the user's profile form.
     */
    public function edit(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('profile.edit', [
            'user' => $request->user(),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's profile information.
     */
    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());

        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
        }

        $request->user()->save();

        return Redirect::route('profile.edit')->with('status', 'profile-updated');
    }

    /**
     * Delete the user's account.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validateWithBag('userDeletion', [
            'password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
        ]);

        $user = $request->user();

        Auth::logout();

        $user->delete();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return Redirect::to('/');
    }
}

?app/Http/Controllers/Student/ApplicationController.php3.0 KB
<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Student;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Application;
use App\Models\Offer;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ApplicationController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;
        
        // Removed 'offers' from with() because offers are linked by student_id, not application_id
        $applications = $student->applications()
            ->with(['job.company', 'interviews'])
            ->latest()
            ->paginate(10);

        // Get all active offers for this student
        $offers = Offer::where('student_id', $student->id)
            ->where('status', 'released')
            ->with(['company', 'job'])
            ->get();

        return view('student.applications.index', compact('applications', 'offers'));
    }

    public function respondToOffer(Request $request, Offer $offer)
    {
        if ($offer->student_id !== auth()->user()->studentProfile->id) { abort(403); }
        if ($offer->status !== 'released') { return back()->with('error', 'This offer is no longer active.'); }

        $request->validate(['action' => 'required|in:accept,reject']);

        if ($request->action === 'accept') {
            $offer->update(['status' => 'accepted', 'responded_at' => now()]);
            
            // Update application status
            Application::where('student_id', $offer->student_id)
                       ->where('job_id', $offer->job_id)
                       ->update(['status' => 'selected']);
                       
            return back()->with('success', 'Congratulations! You have accepted the offer.');
        } else {
            $offer->update(['status' => 'rejected', 'responded_at' => now()]);
            return back()->with('success', 'You have declined the offer.');
        }
    }
    public function withdraw(Application $application)
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;
        
        // Security check
        if ($application->student_id !== $student->id) { abort(403); }
        
        // Check if deadline has passed
        if (\Carbon\Carbon::parse($application->job->deadline)->isPast()) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Cannot withdraw. The application deadline has passed.');
        }

        // Check if already selected
        if (in_array($application->status, ['selected', 'rejected', 'withdrawn'])) {
            return back()->with('error', 'You cannot withdraw this application at its current status.');
        }

        $application->update(['status' => 'withdrawn']);

        // Notify Company
        \App\Models\Notification::create([
            'user_id' => $application->job->company->user_id,
            'title' => 'Application Withdrawn',
            'message' => $student->user->name . ' has withdrawn their application for ' . $application->job->title . '.',
            'type' => 'announcement',
            'is_critical' => false,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Application withdrawn successfully.');
    }
}

?app/Http/Controllers/Student/JobController.php3.4 KB
<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Student;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Job;
use App\Models\Application;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class JobController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;
        
        $jobs = Job::whereHas('colleges', function($query) use ($student) {
            $query->where('college_id', $student->college_id)
                  ->where('approval_status', 'approved');
        })->where('status', 'active')->latest()->paginate(10);

        return view('student.jobs.index', compact('jobs', 'student'));
    }

    public function apply(Request $request, Job $job)
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;

        // 1. Check if already applied
        $exists = Application::where('student_id', $student->id)->where('job_id', $job->id)->exists();
        if ($exists) {
            return back()->with('error', 'You have already applied to this job.');
        }

        // 2. Advanced Eligibility Check
        $eligibility = $job->eligibility;
        if ($eligibility) {
            // CGPA Check
            if (isset($eligibility['cgpa']) && $student->cgpa < $eligibility['cgpa']) {
                return back()->with('error', "Ineligible: Required CGPA is {$eligibility['cgpa']}, yours is {$student->cgpa}.");
            }
            
            // Backlogs Check
            if (isset($eligibility['backlogs']) && $student->backlogs > $eligibility['backlogs']) {
                return back()->with('error', "Ineligible: Maximum allowed backlogs is {$eligibility['backlogs']}.");
            }

            // Passing Year Check
            if (!empty($eligibility['passing_year']) && $student->passing_year != $eligibility['passing_year']) {
                return back()->with('error', "Ineligible: This job is for the {$eligibility['passing_year']} passing year.");
            }

            // Department Check
            if (!empty($eligibility['departments'])) {
                $studentDept = $student->department->code;
                if (!in_array($studentDept, $eligibility['departments'])) {
                    return back()->with('error', "Ineligible: Your department ({$studentDept}) is not allowed for this job.");
                }
            }

            // Skills Check
            if (!empty($eligibility['skills'])) {
                $studentSkills = array_map('strtolower', $student->skills ?? []);
                foreach ($eligibility['skills'] as $reqSkill) {
                    if (!in_array(strtolower($reqSkill), $studentSkills)) {
                        return back()->with('error', "Ineligible: You are missing the required skill: {$reqSkill}.");
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Create Application
        Application::create([
            'student_id' => $student->id,
            'job_id' => $job->id,
            'college_id' => $student->college_id,
            'status' => 'applied'
        ]);

        // Notify Company
        \App\Models\Notification::create([
            'user_id' => $job->company->user_id,
            'title' => 'New Application Received',
            'message' => $student->user->name . ' applied for ' . $job->title,
            'type' => 'announcement',
            'is_critical' => false,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Successfully applied for ' . $job->title);
    }
}

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Student;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use App\Models\ResumeVersion;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    public function edit()
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile->load('college', 'department', 'resumes');
        return view('student.profile.edit', compact('student'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request)
    {
        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;
        
        // Removed CGPA, Backlogs, Passing Year from validation
        $request->validate([
            'skills' => 'nullable|string',
            'bio' => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
        ]);

        $student->update([
            'skills' => $request->skills ? array_map('trim', explode(',', $request->skills)) : [],
            'bio' => $request->bio,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Profile updated successfully. (Academic details must be updated by your TPO).');
    }

    public function uploadResume(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'resume' => 'required|mimes:pdf|max:5120',
        ]);

        $student = auth()->user()->studentProfile;
        $path = $request->file('resume')->store('resumes', 'public');
        ResumeVersion::where('student_id', $student->id)->update(['is_default' => false]);
        $version = ResumeVersion::where('student_id', $student->id)->count() + 1;

        ResumeVersion::create([
            'student_id' => $student->id,
            'resume_url' => $path,
            'version' => $version,
            'is_default' => true,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Resume uploaded successfully (Version ' . $version . ').');
    }
}

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\SuperAdmin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\Company;
use App\Models\BlacklistCollege;
use App\Models\BlacklistCompany;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class BlacklistController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $blacklistedColleges = BlacklistCollege::with('college')->latest()->get();
        $blacklistedCompanies = BlacklistCompany::with('company')->where('scope', 'national')->latest()->get();
        
        return view('super-admin.blacklist.index', compact('blacklistedColleges', 'blacklistedCompanies'));
    }

    public function blacklistCollege(Request $request, College $college)
    {
        $request->validate(['reason' => 'required|string|min:10']);
        
        $college->update(['status' => 'blacklisted']);
        BlacklistCollege::create([
            'college_id' => $college->id,
            'reason' => $request->reason,
            'blacklisted_by' => auth()->id()
        ]);

        \App\Models\AuditLog::create(['user_id' => auth()->id(), 'action' => 'College Blacklisted', 'entity_type' => 'College', 'entity_id' => $college->id, 'ip_address' => request()->ip()]);

        return back()->with('success', 'College blacklisted successfully.');
    }

    public function blacklistCompany(Request $request, Company $company)
    {
        $request->validate(['reason' => 'required|string|min:10']);
        
        $company->update(['status' => 'blacklisted']);
        BlacklistCompany::create([
            'company_id' => $company->id,
            'reason' => $request->reason,
            'scope' => 'national',
            'blacklisted_by' => auth()->id()
        ]);

        \App\Models\AuditLog::create(['user_id' => auth()->id(), 'action' => 'Company Blacklisted', 'entity_type' => 'Company', 'entity_id' => $company->id, 'ip_address' => request()->ip()]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Company blacklisted nationally successfully.');
    }
}
?app/Http/Controllers/SuperAdmin/CollegeVerificationController.php2.2 KB
<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\SuperAdmin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\CollegeAdmin;
use App\Models\Department;
use App\Models\AicteVerification;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class CollegeVerificationController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $colleges = College::with(['state', 'aicteVerifications'])->latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('super-admin.colleges.index', compact('colleges'));
    }

    public function approve(College $college)
    {
        DB::transaction(function () use ($college) {
            $college->update([
                'status' => 'verified',
                'verified_by' => auth()->id(),
                'verified_at' => now(),
            ]);

            $user = User::where('email', $college->email)->first();
            if ($user) {
                CollegeAdmin::firstOrCreate([
                    'user_id' => $user->id,
                    'college_id' => $college->id,
                ], [
                    'designation' => 'TPO',
                ]);
            }

            if ($college->departments()->count() === 0) {
                Department::create(['college_id' => $college->id, 'name' => 'Computer Science', 'code' => 'CSE']);
                Department::create(['college_id' => $college->id, 'name' => 'Information Technology', 'code' => 'IT']);
            }

            \App\Models\AuditLog::create(['user_id' => auth()->id(), 'action' => 'College Verified', 'entity_type' => 'College', 'entity_id' => $college->id, 'ip_address' => request()->ip()]);
        });

        return back()->with('success', 'College verified successfully and Admin account activated.');
    }

    public function reject(College $college)
    {
        $college->update([
            'status' => 'rejected',
            'verified_by' => auth()->id(),
            'verified_at' => now(),
        ]);

        \App\Models\AuditLog::create(['user_id' => auth()->id(), 'action' => 'College Rejected', 'entity_type' => 'College', 'entity_id' => $college->id, 'ip_address' => request()->ip()]);

        return back()->with('error', 'College rejected.');
    }
}

?app/Http/Controllers/SuperAdmin/ReportController.php1.6 KB
<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\SuperAdmin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\PlacementRecord;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\Student;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ReportController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        // Overall Stats
        $totalPlaced = PlacementRecord::count();
        $highestPackage = PlacementRecord::max('package') ?? 0;
        $averagePackage = PlacementRecord::avg('package') ?? 0;
        
        // State-wise Aggregation
        $stateStats = College::withCount(['students'])
            ->with('state')
            ->get()
            ->groupBy('state.name')
            ->map(function ($colleges, $stateName) {
                $studentIds = $colleges->pluck('students')->flatten()->pluck('id');
                $placed = PlacementRecord::whereIn('student_id', $studentIds)->count();
                $avgPackage = PlacementRecord::whereIn('student_id', $studentIds)->avg('package') ?? 0;
                
                return [
                    'state' => $stateName,
                    'total_colleges' => $colleges->count(),
                    'total_students' => $studentIds->count(),
                    'placed_students' => $placed,
                    'placement_rate' => $studentIds->count() > 0 ? round(($placed / $studentIds->count()) * 100, 2) : 0,
                    'avg_package' => round($avgPackage, 2)
                ];
            });

        return view('super-admin.reports.index', compact('totalPlaced', 'highestPackage', 'averagePackage', 'stateStats'));
    }
}
?app/Http/Middleware/RoleMiddleware.php448 B
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class RoleMiddleware
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next, string $role): Response
    {
        if (!auth()->check() || auth()->user()->role !== $role) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized access. You do not have the correct permissions.');
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}
?app/Http/Requests/Auth/LoginRequest.php2.3 KB
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Auth;

use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Lockout;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class LoginRequest extends FormRequest
{
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    public function authenticate(): void
    {
        $this->ensureIsNotRateLimited();

        if (! Auth::attempt($this->only('email', 'password'), $this->boolean('remember'))) {
            RateLimiter::hit($this->throttleKey());

            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => trans('auth.failed'),
            ]);
        }

        // BLACKLIST & APPROVAL CHECK
        if (Auth::user()->status === 'suspended' || Auth::user()->status === 'blacklisted') {
            Auth::logout();
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => 'Your account has been suspended or blacklisted. Please contact support.',
            ]);
        }

        if (Auth::user()->role === 'college_admin') {
            $profile = Auth::user()->collegeAdminProfile;
            if (!$profile) {
                Auth::logout();
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                    'email' => 'Your college registration is still pending Super Admin approval.',
                ]);
            }
        }

        RateLimiter::clear($this->throttleKey());
    }

    public function ensureIsNotRateLimited(): void
    {
        if (! RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($this->throttleKey(), 5)) {
            return;
        }

        event(new Lockout($this));

        $seconds = RateLimiter::availableIn($this->throttleKey());

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => trans('auth.throttle', [
                'seconds' => $seconds,
                'minutes' => ceil($seconds / 60),
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    public function throttleKey(): string
    {
        return Str::transliterate(Str::lower($this->string('email')).'|'.$this->ip());
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

{
    use HasFactory, Notifiable, SoftDeletes;

    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
        'role',
        'phone',
        'status',
    ];

    ];

    {
        return [

            'password' => 'hashed',
        ];
    }

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

            if (empty($model->uuid)) {
                $model->uuid = (string) Str::uuid();

        });

    |

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    |

    |
    */

    'timezone' => 'UTC',

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

?config/auth.php3.9 KB
<?php

use App\Models\User;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Defaults
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option defines the default authentication "guard" and password
    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    |
    */

    'defaults' => [
        'guard' => env('AUTH_GUARD', 'web'),
        'passwords' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_BROKER', 'users'),
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    | which utilizes session storage plus the Eloquent user provider.
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | Supported: "session"
    |
    */

    'guards' => [
        'web' => [
            'driver' => 'session',
            'provider' => 'users',
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User Providers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    | providers to represent the model / table. These providers may then
    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    |
    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    |
    */

    'providers' => [
        'users' => [
            'driver' => 'eloquent',
            'model' => env('AUTH_MODEL', User::class),
        ],

        // 'users' => [
        //     'driver' => 'database',
        //     'table' => 'users',
        // ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Resetting Passwords
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | These configuration options specify the behavior of Laravel's password
    | reset functionality, including the table utilized for token storage
    | and the user provider that is invoked to actually retrieve users.
    |
    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    |
    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    |
    */

    'passwords' => [
        'users' => [
            'provider' => 'users',
            'table' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TABLE', 'password_reset_tokens'),
            'expire' => 60,
            'throttle' => 60,
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Password Confirmation Timeout
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may define the number of seconds before a password confirmation
    | window expires and users are asked to re-enter their password via the
    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
    |
    */

    'password_timeout' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_TIMEOUT', 10800),

];
?config/cache.php3.6 KB
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Cache Store
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option controls the default cache store that will be used by the
    | framework. This connection is utilized if another isn't explicitly
    | specified when running a cache operation inside the application.
    |
    */

    'default' => env('CACHE_STORE', 'database'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Cache Stores
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may define all of the cache "stores" for your application as
    | well as their drivers. You may even define multiple stores for the
    | same cache driver to group types of items stored in your caches.
    |
    | Supported drivers: "array", "database", "file", "memcached",
    |                    "redis", "dynamodb", "octane",
    |                    "failover", "null"
    |
    */

    'stores' => [

        'array' => [
            'driver' => 'array',
            'serialize' => false,
        ],

    |

?database/factories/UserFactory.php1.0 KB
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * @extends Factory<User>
 */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    /**
     * The current password being used by the factory.
     */
    protected static ?string $password;

    /**
     * Define the model's default state.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'email_verified_at' => now(),
            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
     */
    public function unverified(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\State;
use App\Models\College;
use App\Models\Department;
use App\Models\CollegeAdmin;
use App\Models\Student;
use App\Models\Company;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;

class DatabaseSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        // 1. Create States
        $mh = State::create(['name' => 'Maharashtra', 'code' => 'MH']);
        $dl = State::create(['name' => 'Delhi', 'code' => 'DL']);
        State::create(['name' => 'Karnataka', 'code' => 'KA']);
        State::create(['name' => 'Uttar Pradesh', 'code' => 'UP']);
        State::create(['name' => 'Tamil Nadu', 'code' => 'TN']);

        // 2. Create Colleges
        $colA = College::create(['state_id' => $mh->id, 'name' => 'College A', 'code' => 'MH001', 'email' => 'info@collegea.edu', 'status' => 'verified']);
        $colB = College::create(['state_id' => $dl->id, 'name' => 'College B', 'code' => 'DL001', 'email' => 'info@collegeb.edu', 'status' => 'verified']);

        // 3. Create Departments
        $cse = Department::create(['college_id' => $colA->id, 'name' => 'Computer Science', 'code' => 'CSE']);
        $it = Department::create(['college_id' => $colA->id, 'name' => 'Information Technology', 'code' => 'IT']);

        // 4. Create Super Admin
        User::create(['name' => 'Super Admin', 'email' => 'super@campusplace.in', 'password' => Hash::make('Admin@123'), 'role' => 'super_admin', 'status' => 'active']);

        // 5. Create College Admin
        $caUser = User::create(['name' => 'College Admin A', 'email' => 'admin.a@collegea.edu', 'password' => Hash::make('Admin@123'), 'role' => 'college_admin', 'status' => 'active']);
        CollegeAdmin::create(['user_id' => $caUser->id, 'college_id' => $colA->id, 'designation' => 'TPO']);

        // 6. Create Student
        $stUser = User::create(['name' => 'Student One', 'email' => 'student1@collegea.edu', 'password' => Hash::make('Student@123'), 'role' => 'student', 'status' => 'active']);
        Student::create([
            'user_id' => $stUser->id, 
            'college_id' => $colA->id, 
            'department_id' => $cse->id, 
            'enrollment_no' => 'A2026001', 
            'cgpa' => 8.5, 
            'backlogs' => 0, 
            'passing_year' => 2026, 
            'skills' => json_encode(['Python', 'Java']), 
            'status' => 'active'
        ]);

        // 7. Create Company
        $coUser = User::create(['name' => 'TechCorp HR', 'email' => 'hr@techcorp.com', 'password' => Hash::make('Company@123'), 'role' => 'company', 'status' => 'active']);
        Company::create(['user_id' => $coUser->id, 'name' => 'TechCorp', 'status' => 'approved']);
    }
}

?public/.htaccess740 B
<IfModule mod_rewrite.c>
    <IfModule mod_negotiation.c>
        Options -MultiViews -Indexes
    </IfModule>

    RewriteEngine On

    # Handle Authorization Header
    RewriteCond %{HTTP:Authorization} .
    RewriteRule .* - [E=HTTP_AUTHORIZATION:%{HTTP:Authorization}]

    # Handle X-XSRF-Token Header
    RewriteCond %{HTTP:x-xsrf-token} .
    RewriteRule .* - [E=HTTP_X_XSRF_TOKEN:%{HTTP:X-XSRF-Token}]

    # Redirect Trailing Slashes If Not A Folder...
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-d
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_URI} (.+)/$
    RewriteRule ^ %1 [L,R=301]

    # Send Requests To Front Controller...
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-d
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-f
    RewriteRule ^ index.php [L]
</IfModule>

    '2xl' => 'sm:max-w-2xl',
][$maxWidth];

<div
    x-data="{
        show: @js($show),
        focusables() {
            // All focusable element types...
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]
                // All non-disabled elements...
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))
        },
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },
    }"
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {
        if (value) {
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');
            {{ $attributes->has('focusable') ? 'setTimeout(() => firstFocusable().focus(), 100)' : '' }}
        } else {
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');
        }
    })"
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = true : null"
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = false : null"
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"
    x-show="show"
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"
    style="display: {{ $show ? 'block' : 'none' }};"
>
    <div
        x-show="show"
        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"
        x-on:click="show = false"
        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"

    >
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500 opacity-75"></div>

<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\AuthenticatedSessionController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\ConfirmablePasswordController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationNotificationController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationPromptController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\NewPasswordController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordResetLinkController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\VerifyEmailController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::middleware('guest')->group(function () {
    // Default register routes are REMOVED to prevent conflicts with our Custom Hub

    Route::get('login', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'create'])
                ->name('login');

    Route::post('login', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'store']);

    Route::get('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'create'])
                ->name('password.request');

    Route::post('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'store'])
                ->name('password.email');

    Route::get('reset-password/{token}', [NewPasswordController::class, 'create'])
                ->name('password.reset');

    Route::post('reset-password', [NewPasswordController::class, 'store'])
                ->name('password.store');
});

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::get('verify-email', EmailVerificationPromptController::class)
                ->name('verification.notice');

    Route::get('verify-email/{id}/{hash}', VerifyEmailController::class)
                ->middleware(['signed', 'throttle:6,1'])
                ->name('verification.verify');

    Route::post('email/verification-notification', [EmailVerificationNotificationController::class, 'store'])
                ->middleware('throttle:6,1')
                ->name('verification.send');

    Route::get('confirm-password', [ConfirmablePasswordController::class, 'show'])
                ->name('password.confirm');

    Route::post('confirm-password', [ConfirmablePasswordController::class, 'store']);

    Route::put('password', [PasswordController::class, 'update'])->name('password.update');

    Route::post('logout', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'destroy'])
                ->name('logout');
});

// Quick Demo Login
Route::get('/demo-login/{role}', function ($role) {
    $emails = [
        'super_admin' => 'super@campusplace.in',
        'college_admin' => 'admin.a@collegea.edu',
        'student' => 'student1@collegea.edu',
        'company' => 'hr@techcorp.com',
    ];
    if (!array_key_exists($role, $emails)) abort(404);
    $user = \App\Models\User::where('email', $emails[$role])->first();
    if ($user) {
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth::login($user);
        return redirect()->route('dashboard');
    }
    return redirect()->route('login')->with('error', 'Demo user not found.');
})->name('demo.login');


// Force Logout (Bypass loops)
Route::get('/force-logout', function () {
    \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth::logout();
    request()->session()->invalidate();
    request()->session()->regenerateToken();
    return redirect('/login');
})->name('force.logout');

?tests/Feature/Auth/AuthenticationTest.php1.2 KB
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class AuthenticationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_login_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $response = $this->get('/login');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_users_can_authenticate_using_the_login_screen(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->post('/login', [
            'email' => $user->email,
            'password' => 'password',
        ]);

        $this->assertAuthenticated();
        $response->assertRedirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    public function test_users_can_not_authenticate_with_invalid_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/login', [
            'email' => $user->email,
            'password' => 'wrong-password',
        ]);

        $this->assertGuest();
    }

    public function test_users_can_logout(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/logout');

        $this->assertGuest();
        $response->assertRedirect('/');
    }
}
?tests/Feature/Auth/EmailVerificationTest.php1.6 KB
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\URL;
use Tests\TestCase;

class EmailVerificationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_email_verification_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/verify-email');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_email_can_be_verified(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        Event::fake();

        $verificationUrl = URL::temporarySignedRoute(
            'verification.verify',
            now()->addMinutes(60),
            ['id' => $user->id, 'hash' => sha1($user->email)]
        );

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get($verificationUrl);

        Event::assertDispatched(Verified::class);
        $this->assertTrue($user->fresh()->hasVerifiedEmail());
        $response->assertRedirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }

    public function test_email_is_not_verified_with_invalid_hash(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        $verificationUrl = URL::temporarySignedRoute(
            'verification.verify',
            now()->addMinutes(60),
            ['id' => $user->id, 'hash' => sha1('wrong-email')]
        );

        $this->actingAs($user)->get($verificationUrl);

        $this->assertFalse($user->fresh()->hasVerifiedEmail());
    }
}

<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordConfirmationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_confirm_password_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/confirm-password');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_password_can_be_confirmed(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/confirm-password', [
            'password' => 'password',
        ]);

        $response->assertRedirect();
        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
    }

    public function test_password_is_not_confirmed_with_invalid_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/confirm-password', [
            'password' => 'wrong-password',
        ]);

        $response->assertSessionHasErrors();
    }
}
?tests/Feature/Auth/PasswordResetTest.php1.9 KB
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Notifications\ResetPassword;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordResetTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_reset_password_link_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $response = $this->get('/forgot-password');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_reset_password_link_can_be_requested(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class);
    }

    public function test_reset_password_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class, function ($notification) {
            $response = $this->get('/reset-password/'.$notification->token);

            $response->assertStatus(200);

            return true;
        });
    }

    public function test_password_can_be_reset_with_valid_token(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class, function ($notification) use ($user) {
            $response = $this->post('/reset-password', [
                'token' => $notification->token,
                'email' => $user->email,
                'password' => 'password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'password',
            ]);

            $response
                ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
                ->assertRedirect(route('login'));

            return true;
        });
    }
}
?tests/Feature/Auth/PasswordUpdateTest.php1.4 KB
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordUpdateTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_password_can_be_updated(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->put('/password', [
                'current_password' => 'password',
                'password' => 'new-password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'new-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertTrue(Hash::check('new-password', $user->refresh()->password));
    }

    public function test_correct_password_must_be_provided_to_update_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->put('/password', [
                'current_password' => 'wrong-password',
                'password' => 'new-password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'new-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasErrorsIn('updatePassword', 'current_password')
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');
    }
}

?tests/Feature/ProfileTest.php2.4 KB
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class ProfileTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_profile_page_is_displayed(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->get('/profile');

        $response->assertOk();
    }

    public function test_profile_information_can_be_updated(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->patch('/profile', [
                'name' => 'Test User',
                'email' => 'test@example.com',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $user->refresh();

        $this->assertSame('Test User', $user->name);
        $this->assertSame('test@example.com', $user->email);
        $this->assertNull($user->email_verified_at);
    }

    public function test_email_verification_status_is_unchanged_when_the_email_address_is_unchanged(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->patch('/profile', [
                'name' => 'Test User',
                'email' => $user->email,
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertNotNull($user->refresh()->email_verified_at);
    }

    public function test_user_can_delete_their_account(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->delete('/profile', [
                'password' => 'password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/');

        $this->assertGuest();
        $this->assertNull($user->fresh());
    }

    public function test_correct_password_must_be_provided_to_delete_account(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->delete('/profile', [
                'password' => 'wrong-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasErrorsIn('userDeletion', 'password')
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertNotNull($user->fresh());
    }
}
